## 8. REG-11 Gate B — Reports Owner-Binding Investigation (`2026-10-13`)

### 8.1 Bounded result

| Gate B question | Result |
|---|---|
| F-3 `(tenantId, ownerUserId)` → Reports `(tenantId, userId)` | **NOT PROVEN.** Direct field copying conflicts with the F-3 value semantics; no D-2 §8 Reports translation or Reports-specific no-auth owner contract exists. |
| Persistence boundary | **PROVEN technical fit only.** D-1 designates the NP-04 artifact/report domain at pin `2e11fa3…`; this does not authorize Reports package consumption/admission or establish identity. |
| D-1 / PR #42 (`P1`) | Historical merge-authority conflict remains **UNRESOLVED**. No retrospective approval or remediation. |
| IPD act `26061859…` | **Does not cover PR #42** under its explicit source/target/exclusion scope. Coverage by any different act remains **NOT ESTABLISHED**. The act's `6828155…` “NP04” label conflicts with D-1/AD-20's G24 portfolio-lineage designation; no relabeling is inferred. |
| G2-2 `AD-01` | Candidate record's operative effect is **NOT ESTABLISHED**; the 13-path historical presence fact is not an admission. This corrects the earlier tracker wording. |
| F-4 / F-7 | Reports remains **DEFERRED** under effective F-3 scope; F-7 qualification for the F-3/no-auth Reports path is **NOT SATISFIED**. Existing authenticated Reports evidence is scope-limited; restart status is conflicting/conditional under D-3. |
| Route, runtime, UI | Current source returns `503 owner-context-unavailable` before domain/persistence access. Live runtime configuration is **UNPROVEN**. No Reports UI/no-auth port was found at current `main` or the exact accepted D-3 branch tree; no UI admission. |
| Application code | **Zero changes.** Reports and User Portfolios remain fail-closed. |

### 8.2 Classification discipline

`PROVEN` means direct evidence at the cited ref; `DECIDED` means an effective governing record; `INFERRED` is not used to create any identity or authority; `UNPROVEN` means evidence is absent or insufficient; `CONFLICTING` records incompatible source/status claims without resolving them by assumption; `DEFERRED` means the governing F-3 scope excludes the capability for now. Persistence fit, identity binding, qualification, implementation authority, acceptance, product admission, route/UI enablement, publication, and remote verification remain distinct layers.

### 8.3 Reconciled D-1 / PR #42 / act status

D-1 designates NP-04 `./persistence` for the artifact/report domain but grants no implementation, promotion, main-admission, package/API, or identity-mapping authority. PR #42 merged 23 paths (13 Reports backend/domain/test paths plus 10 shared/package/auth/tenant/PIT paths) at `47edf6f3…`, despite its title/body requiring a separate promotion act. The chosen store is technically consistent with D-1; the merge and package edge are not authorized by that designation. This is the still-open P1 conflict.

Act `2606185923f6cbd3f4df5c3af200f54d40ed4bbc` names source `6828155…` and target `arena/01a0e6d9-iips-production-market-data`, expressly withholds `main` and Reports, and contains no IRR PR #42 authorization. Under AD-20, `2e11fa3…` is the artifact/report pin while `6828155…` is the G24 portfolio/tenancy reference; the act's conflicting “NP04 Phase-A” label is recorded without changing either lineage. Other-act coverage remains **NOT ESTABLISHED**.

The G2-2 file at blob `2f89bbc9…` says it is a candidate, not approved/published/authoritative, with independent verification still required. PR #51 merged it under a “DRAFT: not approved; do not merge” title; PR #53 records post-merge default checks `32 pass / 1 fail / 2 not verified` (C01 failed after base drift; C14/C15 not verified). The merged bytes prove a candidate was placed on `main`; they do not prove operative AD-01 effect. Historical files are preserved unchanged.

### 8.4 F-4 / F-7 and Reports evidence

The effective F-3 mechanism decision (PR #68) keeps Reports and Portfolios deferred and unconditional `503`; its implementation scope is NP-09/10/11 only. NP-06-R1 on the accepted Reports branch authorized the then-authenticated principal-based Reports implementation, not an F-3-to-Reports mapping or replacement of the established authentication architecture. D-3 accepts only that qualified branch scope and grants no main admission.

The accepted branch's qualification reports `32/32` requirements and `228/228` tracked tests for the authenticated-principal implementation. D-3 separately leaves process restart/recovery and live-IdP evidence conditional/unproven because the execution harness was not committed. Neither record qualifies the F-3 pair. F-7 remains unmet for positive/negative F-3 ownership tests and reproducible separate-process durability; current fail-closed behavior alone is insufficient. See the full per-part classification in the [Gate B investigation record](docs/integration/IIPS_v3.0_GATE_B_REPORTS_OWNER_BINDING_INVESTIGATION_2026-10-13.md) §6.

The earlier main-tracker claim that `Reports.tsx` exists at `arena/01a0f1b3@6a8afbb2…` is not reproducible: the complete remote tree (1,265 entries; not truncated) contains no Reports UI component. Current `main` and PR #42's file manifest likewise contain no Reports UI path. D-3 UI08 exclusion remains.

### 8.5 Minimum next governance action

The first substantive next step is a PA decision choosing either a Reports-specific non-auth owner contract or a D-2 §8 translation contract with source/target authorities, identifier semantics, lifecycle, provisioning/revocation, tenant/ownership/authorization boundaries, audit, failure behavior, trust, durable storage, and package/API contract. P1 remains an independent open issue. Only after these decisions may separately authorized implementation, F-7 evidence, Reports/UI acceptance, product admission, or route/UI activation be considered. Keep Gate A separate; it is not a Gate B prerequisite. Preserve the 503 fence.

| Channel / check | Result |
|---|---|
| IRR local | Initially clean shallow checkout at `39d03d6…`; source files read directly. |
| Remote IRR | Current `main`, accepted Reports branch tree, PR #42/#51/#53 state and manifests queried through GitHub. |

| Windows / PowerShell | Not accessed; no Windows-side result is claimed. |
| Application tests / runtime probes | Not run. Existing Reports 503 test assertions and source path inspected only; no server, environment value, database, or user data accessed. |
| Documentation validation | `git diff --check` and the two-file documentation-only scope check are performed before publication. |

## 10. REG-11 Gate B — Reports Ownership Source (R1) and Contract Shape (R2) PA Decision Request (`2026-10-13`)

*Additive decision-request section for `REG-11` / `Gate B`. Operating record: [`docs/integration/IIPS_v3.0_GATE_B_REPORTS_OWNERSHIP_SOURCE_AND_CONTRACT_SHAPE_PA_DECISION_REQUEST_2026-10-13.md`](docs/integration/IIPS_v3.0_GATE_B_REPORTS_OWNERSHIP_SOURCE_AND_CONTRACT_SHAPE_PA_DECISION_REQUEST_2026-10-13.md) (record ID `IIPS-V3-REG11-GATE-B-R1-R2-PA-DECISION-REQUEST-2026-10-13-01`). **PA CHOICE = PENDING.** This section is **not** a decision and selects no option; the operating record is authoritative for its own findings and citations.*

### 10.1 Decision requested from the Program Authority

| Input | Question | Status |
|---|---|---|
| **R1 — ownership-source authority** | R1.1 Does any existing authority (IRR- or IPD-side) establish the Reports target's ownership contract and source class — or is it **not established**? R1.2 May a PA-governed **non-auth** declaration lawfully supply `AuthenticatedOwner` at that boundary? R1.3 Does the F-2 tenant value's scope extend to the artifact/report boundary (or is a separate Reports tenant value/act required)? R1.4 If R1.1–R1.3 cannot be resolved, does Reports remain deferred? | **PENDING** |
| **R2 — contract shape** | Choose exactly one: **A** bind the accepted F-1/F-2 pair under a Reports-specific D-2 §8 contract; **B** establish a new Reports-specific declaration + value act; **C** retain deferral. | **PENDING** |

### 10.2 Options — prerequisites and consequences (summary)

| Option | Prerequisites | Consequences | Authority required | Evidence-supported now? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **A** Bind an existing governed identity pair | Target authority located/created (R1.1); authorized non-auth ownership-source class (R1.2); F-2 scope extension authorized (R1.3); full 15-element D-2 §8 act; affirmative no-authorization/no-client-identity/fail-closed statements; R3 decided before enablement | No new values; consistent with `F-4`(1) single-declaration framing; **extends the artifact/report store's ownership semantics** to a declaration-derived source (boundary security change); adapter change remains an implementation act; F-7 still required | PA binding act; target-authority determination (possibly IPD-side) | **No — P-A1/P-A2/P-A3 unestablished** |
| **B** New Reports-specific declaration + value act | Target authority located/created (same R1.1 burden); PA authority for new ownership/security semantics **and** a new value act (or an `F-4`(1) amendment); full element set + pair-relationship statement; R3 decided before enablement | Creates a **second ownership declaration** regime needing express reconciliation with `F-4`(1); same boundary security change as A plus a value-selection act; doubles F-3/F-7 evidentiary surface | PA binding act + new value act; target-authority determination | **No — P-B1/P-B2 unestablished** |
| **C** Retain deferral | **None** | State fully preserved; no new semantics; Gate B stays open; `503` guarantee unchanged; R1/R2 unresolved | None (confirmation only) | **Yes — prerequisites satisfied** |

**Neither A nor B is recommended as supported by current source evidence**; on the evidence as it stands only **C** has satisfied prerequisites. A or B become available only if the PA's R1 determination supplies their prerequisites. No option is selected here.

Structurally compatible field shapes and a store that validates only non-empty members are **technical facts**, not authority. Reaching the store with a non-principal pair requires (i) the R1.2 determination, (ii) a source change under a separate implementation gate, and (iii) R3 for the `./persistence` edge. Equivalences denied by the effective value act §5(7) and D-2 §8 remain **unauthorized** regardless of feasibility.

### 15.5 Recommended minimal contract (preparer's recommendation — non-operative)

**Revised, non-operative recommendation: DP-1 = Option 1D (RETAIN BINDING DEFERRAL); DP-2 = new narrow Option 2F (unresolved evidence + refused writes); DP-3 = 3B (separate admission).** The original 1A′/2A recommendation is superseded, not a revoked PA decision: no PA selection ever occurred. No local issuer or provisioning is already authorized by the existing evidence; compatibility with RS256/JWKS alone does not satisfy the complete delegated-credential/mapping/membership trust model. Binding remains unresolved and integrated reads/writes remain blocked.

**Disposition semantics determined from code, not its name:** `NON_PRODUCTION_OPERATOR_BYPASS` does not bypass credential/membership/owner checks (HTTP verification and authorization precede body parsing). It **does** waive Save Guard 2's strict entity-resolution requirement; G24-C6 tests the accepted/rejected pair at store level, not HTTP credential certification. The original PA-authorized frontend broker-import workflow required environment+enable gates; the G24 durable path lacks that gate. It is therefore **not passive metadata (A)**; it is an operative identity-resolution exception (**B for “another G24 requirement”**, not an auth bypass). **2A is removed from the recommended path.** No conclusion of production safety or durable-path authorization is inferred. Evidence and future safeguards are in packet §5.E.

**2F is independently decidable** as a narrow, fail-closed contract, not canonical identifier selection: preserve source symbol/ISIN/exchange bytes and absence; omit companyId; retain server-governed UNRESOLVED; refuse client identity/disposition claims; no bypass, fake canonical provenance or invented IPD enum; reject unresolved/ambiguous saves before any port/HTTP call. No successful save, mapping or admission is enabled. No ticker-to-companyId mapping, CIA designation, D115-A effectiveness or exchange-composed symbol. Alternatives retained in packet §7.

### 15.6 Independent clauses and recorded standing-authority application

Packet §9.2 separates **GC-D1** (retain binding deferral; no issuer/configuration/provisioning/membership) from **GC-D2** (approve only 2F plus bounded offline IRR contract implementation/qualification). Either clause carries the explicit boundary: no User Portfolios admission, P2/P3 disposition, authentication/authorization or identity-resolution bypass, route/UI activation, G24 promotion, IPD ref change, lineage merge, CIA/equivalence/D115-A effect, or Gate A/B/R3/R4 change. **Original rectification:** neither clause was selected. **Current standing-authority application:** packet §9.4 records GC-D1 = 1D deferral and GC-D2 = 2F approval for S2–S4 only, attributed to the durable F-3 §1.2 standing text (main blob `c4c9f7d…`) plus the current PA direction applying it to these exact clauses. The requested Library path is user-supplied and not retrieved; no signed act or unsupported grant date is fabricated. **EFFECTIVE:** decision-only PR #82 merged at `f01803e987afa80c64ab98232346698e689abd60` (2026-10-13T14:37:58Z); independent remote main/artifact byte verification completed before code edits. ### 15.15 P-2 handoff Block D1 acceptance-criteria correction (`GC-P2-HANDOFF-D1-CORR-01`; recorded `2026-10-14` Asia/Calcutta — `2026-10-13T19:12Z` / `2026-10-14 00:42 IST`)

Correction to §15.14's handoff, arising from the first Windows execution evidence. Record: [`docs/integration/IIPS_v3.0_GATE_C_P2_EXECUTION_HANDOFF_ARENA_TO_NON_PRODUCTION_HOST_2026-10-13.md`](docs/integration/IIPS_v3.0_GATE_C_P2_EXECUTION_HANDOFF_ARENA_TO_NON_PRODUCTION_HOST_2026-10-13.md) §6.4 (revised Block D) and §10.1 (investigation). Acceptance-criteria correction only.

**Reported discrepancy.** Block C passed on the designated host (`npm ci --ignore-scripts` exit 0; `tsc` producing exactly the four expected baseline errors; both build artifacts present) and all five operator-tooling SHA-256 hashes matched the handoff. Block D1 (issuer absent) then produced the launcher preflight refusal — `FAIL: required environment variable IPD_OIDC_ISSUER is absent or blank`, exit **65**, database not created — where the handoff (§6.4, as first published) had required `D1_EXIT=1` with `OidcConfigurationError … OIDC_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. D2 behaved exactly as prescribed (exit 1, `PERSISTENCE_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`, no listener).

**Determination from source evidence.** The discrepancy is in the **handoff**, not in the tooling. The hash-verified launcher (`0a231e63a3c5712a7002d0043f8fb71c818e9cc15aed78e238b885ddce5b37a6`, independently reproduced this session and equal to the operator's verified hash) validates all six required variables and exits 65 **before** `await import(entry)`; the pinned startup path (`src/server/http-server.ts` at `6828155e…`) is `loadIpdHttpConfig → loadOidcTrustConfig → initializePersistence → listen`, so with the issuer absent the runtime module is **never imported** and exit 1 with `OidcConfigurationError` is **unreachable by construction**. The launcher's stricter-than-the-runtime preflight is intended behaviour and is a genuine guarantee, but it is a **different** guarantee from the loader's fail-closed validation; the original pairing conflated the two layers.

**Correction applied (smallest sufficient).** D1's command is unchanged; only its acceptance criteria are restated as the **Layer-1** guarantee (`D1_EXIT=65` + the exact message + `D1_PREFLIGHT_REFUSAL=True` + `D1_DB_CREATED=False` + `D1_LISTENER_8099=False`), and a bounded **D1b** probe was added that calls the pinned entry point directly to observe the **Layer-2** guarantee for three cases (absent issuer, blank issuer, absent JWKS), each expected `D1B OidcConfigurationError OIDC_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` with `exit=0`, no database and no listener. D2 is unchanged and already satisfied — its preflight passes, so it does reach the pinned persistence loader (`PERSISTENCE_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`). The launcher, all five tooling hashes, the state root and the pinned IPD source/build artifacts are **untouched**; D1b adds no tooling file and fails closed before any connection.

Independently decoding the API's base64 content and SHA-256 hashing the raw bytes produced `E5CD7E490FF69BACBB6496E5B6ED0CC29C3FD8786D8DEE04798A2B68D787D4BE`; recomputing Git's `blob <size><NUL><bytes>` SHA-1 reproduced the blob ID. The commit/merge ancestry, manifest contract, destination path and actual bounded coverage are consistent.

**PR disposition.** PR [#96](https://github.com/ramkivs/iips-review-recovered/pull/96), base `main`, head `arena/63a310bc-iips-review-recovered` at the exact transfer commit, was **MERGED** at `2026-10-14T09:20:37Z` as merge commit `f813354092b9e926ad93fb06080a7d64465aa4ae`. **Validation exception — not a pass.** The Windows-reported `git diff --cached --check` failure was investigated and reproduced against the committed patch: standard `git diff --check` exits 2 and reports 315 CRLF-ending lines as trailing whitespace under the default Git whitespace rules. A CRLF-aware diagnostic suppresses those line-ending reports but still detects literal trailing spaces on lines 6, 38 and 99 (`Configuration Name: `). The accepted artifact is a raw PowerShell `Start-Transcript` capture and the amendment requires byte-for-byte deposit; it was not rewritten, normalized, or edited. The failure remains recorded as a validation exception. No application tests are claimed for this evidence-only change.

**Cross-record consistency and preserved fences.** The destination folder contains the amended manifest and this sole artifact. # PART II — CONSOLIDATED CONVERGENCE REGISTER AND FORWARD PLAN (`2026-10-14`, independent re-establishment)

*Prepared in a fresh Arena session on branch `arena/ee3fd321-iips-review-recovered`. Additive consolidation: this section creates **no** new decision, lifts **no** fence, authorizes **no** implementation, and does **not** replace §§1–15 or any governed record. Where this section and §§1–15 disagree on a *decision*, §§1–15 govern; where they disagree on an *observable repository fact*, the live re-verification recorded at §16.3 governs and the earlier text is corrected additively.*

## 16.1 Purpose, method and reading order

**Purpose.** Establish, from the repositories themselves, what the IIPS application is, what is actually complete and verified across IRR and IPD, what is genuinely unfinished, which items actually prevent convergence, what can proceed in parallel, and the shortest safe route to a coherent, usable single-user application.

**Method used in this session.** Read the authoritative records (§§1–15, the `docs/integration` decision corpus, `program-v1.1-certification/`, IPD `docs/`+`evidence/`+ the IPD XLSX program tracker), then independently re-verify the consequential facts against the live repositories and by **executing the application** in this sandbox (fresh `npm ci`, typecheck, full test suite, production build, live HTTP probe of every served route, journal write, and branch-reachability analysis). No prior session's reported state was accepted as proof of current state where the repository could be checked directly.

**Reading order for a newcomer:** §17 (executive view) → §18 (capability matrix) → §19 (pending work) → §20 (ordered progression) for the structured view; and for the underlying record trail §16.2 → §16.3 → §16.4 → §16.5 → §16.6 → §16.7 (§16.7.1 decisions, §16.7.2 per-capability paths) → §16.8 → §16.9–§16.10 (boundaries and limits) → §16.11 → §16.12 → §16.13/§16.14 (corrections applied to this section).

## 16.2 What the application is (plain English)

IIPS is a **personal investment-research and portfolio-intelligence workbench** for a single owner-operator, running **non-production** on that person's own machine. Two repositories, one product:

| Layer | Repository | What it contributes |
|---|---|---|
| Application + certified analytics | `ramkivs/iips-review-recovered` (IRR) | React/Vite UI shell (24 leaf routes; 5 named surfaces plus the wildcard honestly render a "not yet built" placeholder); a first-party HTTP transport (`frontend/server/executive-transport.ts`, default `:8787`) that runs the certified 13-sector engine platform **in-process**; durable owner-scoped surfaces (watchlists, collaboration, settings) on an append-only NDJSON journal; governed screener; evidence/snapshot/replay browsing; AI explanation over certified results |
| Data-plane library + operator console | `ramkivs/iips-production-market-data` (IPD) | Canonical contracts, security master / identity resolution, normalization, quality, point-in-time (PIT) store and read service, fundamentals, intelligence data, broker-CSV import, plus an offline operator UI shell whose server-coupled surfaces are honestly fail-closed |

**Compute model.** Sector engines (IES-006…015, 016, 017, 020 — 13 certified) produce deterministic `EngineOutput`s over a **frozen replay baseline**; the Cross-Sector Intelligence Pipeline composes them; the transport serves 1:1 DTOs. The UI is presentation-only and never recomputes a decision. Reference analytics are labelled `freshness: SNAPSHOT` (`calibratedAt 2026-08-09`).

**Ownership model.** No authentication, no login, no roles, no tenants as a product concept. Durability is partitioned by a **server-owned owner-context carrier** (`IIPS_OWNER_CONTEXT_PATH` → a 363-byte JSON file that must match a pinned SHA-256 and the approved `(owner-7f3a91c2, irr-domain-4b82d6e1)` pair) resolved per request, uncached. If the carrier is absent or invalid, every owner-scoped operation answers HTTP `503 owner-context-unavailable`; nothing is inferred and no fallback owner exists.

### 16.4.4 Blocked (and what the block actually is)

**Accounting rule (`CV-CORR-03`).** Two of the three rows below are **active** blocked work — CV-51 and CV-52 each wait on a named decision and have work sequenced behind them. **CV-50 is terminal**: a path closed by scope, retained so the incompatibility is not rediscovered, with no dependants and nothing scheduled. Its status cell is **not** altered; what changes is only that the pending-work totals in §16.12 and §19 count **active outstanding work (22)** separately from **terminal, scope-closed records (1)**. ## 16.5 Root blockers and dependency relationships

**There is no single root blocker.** Four independent chains exist, plus independent hygiene items. Conflating them is what made the portfolio path look like it blocked the whole application; it does not.

| # | What is blocked | Actual blocker | Not blocked by |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The **G24 implementation path** (`P-4`→`P-6`→`P-8`) | its own trust model vs the corrected scope — a **closed path**, CV-50 | anything the rest of the plan needs |
| 2 | **User Portfolio admission** (the capability, per `G-2`) | first a PA act authorizing **re-qualification** (and, if chosen, a bounded residual implementation act) plus the **non-authentication ownership source** designation; **admission is a further separate act** that alone lifts `D-1`/record §8.5 — CV-20 → CV-52 (readiness/implementation records already exist: V16) | CV-50 (a closed path is not a prerequisite); the runbook, pins, IPD test runner |
| 3 | **Reports admission** | the recorded `REG-11` Option C deferral and its own `R1`/`R2`/`R3`/`F-7` requirements — CV-21 → CV-51 | CV-20, CV-39, CV-50 (`G-2` §8 excludes Reports from the portfolio decision) |
| 4 | **Real-security coverage on durable surfaces** | the governed-universe scope question — CV-22 (identity/security-master domain, resolvable on IPD `main`'s existing `SecurityMaster`/D05 per `G-2` §2) | CV-20, CV-21, CV-50 (store choice cannot widen the universe) |

```
(2) CV-20 G-2 sequencing  ── entry: Stage 4 PA act authorizing qualification (+ optional residual implementation)
      ├── CV-39  broker ingestion durability — input to the qualification act (no separate decision)
      ├── residual implementation act for what remains unbuilt — separate, optional, narrower than admission
      ├── CV-52  P2/P3 admission + AD-20/Gate A — ONLY once the qualification bar is evidenced;
      │         admission is not a Stage 4 option and never waives qualification
      └── (fence) /api/user-portfolios* stays 503 until the last step
            — the seam is 503 *by authorization*, not by absence: the durable adapter exists
              on origin/arena/a2df3b85 (149 behind main) and is unadmitted (V16/V17)
      ✗ CV-50 is NOT upstream of this chain: the G24 path is closed by scope, and nothing waits on it

(3) CV-21 Reports disposition  ── independent gate (REG-11 Option C)
      └── CV-51 Reports enablement (R1/R2 → R3 → F-7 → UI/no-auth port)

(4) CV-22 governed universe / security coverage  ── independent of (2) and (3)
      └── CV-32 PIT UI surface (a display decision; seam already live)

(1) CV-50 closed G24 path  ── terminal record; no dependants

Independent of all four — each needed for a usable application, none needing a decision:
      CV-25 pin durability · CV-30/CV-31 runbook + start command · CV-35 IPD test-runner false green
      CV-33/CV-34/CV-36/CV-37 documentation & tracking integrity · CV-38 off-main restart proof
      CV-23 Gate A necessity (no consumer since CV-09) · CV-24/CV-40/CV-41 small dispositions
```

**Reuse rule.** Nothing above re-opens `REG-01`…`REG-13`, `GC-D1`…`GC-D6`, `AD-01`/`AD-02`/`AD-06`, the Option C deferral, `F-1/F-2/F-3`, or **`G-2`** itself: `CV-20` sequences the accepted decision, `CV-50` records that one implementation of it is closed by scope, and `CV-39` corrects a status attribution (`REG-06` covers Operator Drop, not broker ingestion). `CV-23` asks whether a retained gate is still needed, not whether its evidence was wrong.

## 16.7 Forward plan (dependency-linked work packages)

No predetermined phases, and no single gating decision: WP-1, WP-2 and WP-3 are mutually independent and independent of every decision; **WP-4** holds three *separate* questions (portfolios / Reports / universe); **WP-4b** is the portfolio chain’s entry act — narrowed, on V16 evidence, to the **admission + ownership-source decision** rather than a fresh readiness study; **WP-5** executes only what its own authority acts grant; **WP-6** closes named leftovers. Nothing here re-runs completed evidence — V1–V14 remain the current acceptance evidence for CV-01…CV-06, CV-09 and CV-11 (unchanged inputs, unchanged results); V15 is the only check added by this correction.

### 16.7.1 The precise outstanding questions (no manufactured dependencies; nothing here re-opens `G-2`)

**Q1 (CV-20) — what does the PA authorize for the already-implemented, unadmitted durable portfolio line?**
The three acts in this chain are **separate and ordered**: (i) authorization to work the evidence — qualification (re-verification) and, if wanted, a bounded residual implementation act; (ii) the recorded **qualification** result; (iii) a later, distinct **admission** act naming commits and boundaries. Q1 is only (i) plus the ownership-source designation — it cannot grant (iii) by implication, and no option below does.
- **A. Authorize the qualification sequence only (the recommended default).** *Content:* re-qualify the durable line (`470cc698…`) at the rebased tree — its recorded evidence is targeted `59/59` and a full-suite "+59 with zero new failures" measured against the 2026-10-07 baseline, and the branch is **`149` commits behind** `main` (V17) — plus the executed restart-durability and idempotence proofs `G-2` §3 demands. *Consequence:* the admission decision then rests on current evidence; nothing is admitted, pinned, promoted or un-fenced by this act, and it authorizes no product-code change of any kind — implementation would require option B.²
- **B. Authorize A *and* a bounded residual implementation act.** *Content:* the same qualification mandate plus authority to implement only what the records leave unbuilt — durability behind IPD `main`’s in-memory `PortfolioStore` (CV-39) and the wiring of an admitted store, each inside the `G-2` §5 additive boundary. *Consequence:* closes the gap in one planning pass, but implementation authority stays narrower than admission: qualification (ii) must still be recorded, and **admission (iii) remains a separate later act**.²
- **C. Expressly defer, with a dated reason.** *Consequence:* the read surfaces stay fail-closed and are described as **implemented-but-unadmitted** (not "unbuilt"); WP-5 stays closed; nothing else in the plan is affected. Honest and complete on its own; the only cost is that owner-owned holdings stay fenced this cycle.
- **Deliberately not offered: "admit as-is" with a dated acceptance note.** The records leave no such shortcut. `D-1` keeps G24 main admission **prohibited**, the implementation record §8.5 withholds admission of its own act, and `AD-20`/Gate A with `P2`/`P3` hold a qualification bar that an admission act is required to rest on — so admitting `470cc698…` today would either bypass qualification or silently grant authority the records reserve separately, on a stale tree inheriting two failures `main` has since moved past. If the PA ever chooses admission, it is issued **after** (ii) as its own act naming the commits, the boundaries, the fence state and the qualification evidence it relies on.
- **Separable second act (accompanies A, B or C):** designate the **non-authentication ownership source** the `503` seam waits on — `frontend/server/user-portfolio-transport.ts` states it does not authenticate, synthesize a principal, pass credentials upstream or infer a tenant, and the implementation record §8.1–§8.2 deferrals are what keep the seam closed. Naming that source is a small, in-scope act and does **not** admit any lineage; **re-adding OIDC/Keycloak machinery to make the branch store admissible is not an option** (CV-50, terminal).
- **Not options at all:** selecting an IRR-owned portfolio store, reading `G-2` acceptance as implementation or admission authority (`G-2` §1/§5/§8/§9), or reviving the retired trust-anchor chain. Any change of *ownership* would require a subsequent, equally explicit PA architectural act, which this plan neither proposes nor assumes.

² A or B authorizes **evidence-gathering and, under B, narrower implementation**; neither is an admission act. Stage 5 in §20 states the exit criteria for each step separately.

**Q1a (CV-20) — within IPD ownership, which durable storage technology satisfies `G-2` §3?** Reserved to the PA, and properly **answered by the Stage 5 qualification evidence** (not by a guess now, and not by `WP-4b` — whose readiness output already exists on `main`, V16). `G-2` deliberately leaves storage technology unchosen; `6828155e` remains an `AD-03` reference designation, not an adopted store, and its OIDC-shaped trust model is out of scope (CV-50).

**Q1b (CV-20, XV-13) — how are the `G-2` §3 requirements read for a single-owner, non-production application?** Specifically "explicit owner/principal identity", "explicit tenant/company context", "server-side authorization" and "tenant and owner isolation": whether the already-governed F-3 partition semantics satisfy them (as they do for `NP-09/10/11`) or whether a differently stated in-scope mechanism is required. The **requirements themselves stay**; only their in-scope realization is open.

**Q2 (CV-21) — Reports:** retire from the v1 surface set / commission a bounded first-party design / retain deferral. Independent of Q1 by `G-2` §8; answering Q1 answers nothing here.

**Q3 (CV-22) — Governed universe:** keep the 13-row universe / widen durable rows to the governed D05 2,250-entity master already exposed on IPD `main` (real coverage 52) / commission real market coverage (outside non-production scope). Independent of store choice.

**Q4 (CV-23) — Gate A necessity**; **Q5 (CV-25) — pin tagging (reproducibility, independent)**; **Q6 (CV-24) — `F6` inventory and `TD-7b`**.

**Work that may proceed now, regardless of Q1–Q6:** WP-1, WP-2 (tag approval only), WP-3, and CV-21/CV-22 documentation of their own status. **Work that must not:** any `P-4`/`P-6`/`P-8` step (CV-50 — closed path, not a to-do); implementation on the `G-2` path before its §9 readiness act and its own authority; IPD `main` promotion, re-pin or lineage merge; activation of `/api/user-portfolios*` or `/api/reports*`; any merge of the stale auth-era branches (XV-10).

### 16.7.2 Critical path and the smallest materially-advancing set

**Per-capability critical paths (there is no single one for "the application"):**
- *Coherent, usable today:* **WP-1 → WP-3 → WP-2** (runbook + start command + integrity + pin protection). No decision required; this is the whole distance between "verified repository" and "usable application" for everything that already works.
- *User Portfolios:* **Q1 (authorize the qualification act; a bounded residual implementation act only if chosen) → recorded qualification result → admission as its own later act** (`P2`/`P3`-equivalent, CV-52) → ownership-source designation and read-surface enablement, in that order (CV-20 → CV-39 → CV-52; V16/V17). Design and the non-production implementation are **not** outstanding — what is outstanding is qualification **at the current tree** and then admission, which no act in this register grants and no earlier option in this plan substitutes.
- *Reports:* **Q2 → R1/R2 → R3 → F-7 → UI port** (CV-21 → CV-51) — parallel to, never downstream of, the portfolio chain.
- *Real-security coverage:* **Q3** alone (CV-22) — independent of both, and the cheapest large usability gain.

**Smallest set that materially advances convergence:** the **WP-1 + WP-3 + WP-2 bundle** plus **one dated line per question Q1/Q2/Q3/Q5**. That bundle makes the existing verified product operable and trustworthy by a newcomer, and Q1–Q3 either close three capability questions or record them as deliberate boundaries. It is intentionally not "finish the portfolio chain": `G-2` §9 makes that a multi-act sequence, and no part of it may be compressed by an implementer.

## 16.8 Concrete acceptance criteria for "converged and usable" (product level)

1. Fresh clone → `npm ci` → `npm run build` → `tsc --noEmit` 0 → `vitest run` **703/703** (or the then-current count) with no manual source edits. *(Already met: V1–V4.)*
2. A newcomer following only the runbook starts the transport and UI and sees the 13-engine reference analytics, screener execution, evidence/replay, and can create + read a durable watchlist. *(Met once the runbook exists: V5, V6, V8 + CV-30.)*
3. Owner-scoped failure semantics remain honest and are *shown* as such: absent/invalid carrier → `503`, never a fabricated owner. *(Met: V7.)*
4. The consumed pin and the `AD-03` reference resolve to **tagged** commits; install reproducibility documented. *(CV-25 — protects every path above; it is not a prerequisite of any of them.)*
5. IPD `npm test` on a clean tree yields a real run or a non-zero exit. *(CV-35.)*
6. **User-portfolio coherence, expressed as `G-2` compliance rather than as a store choice:** either the fenced state is *stated in the UI and in this register* as a deliberate product boundary, or the `G-2` path is completed through its own acts **in order** — qualification recorded at the current tree, then admission by a separate act — and demonstrates every §3 element it claims — explicit owner scoping, stable portfolio identity, restart-durable persistence, versioned serialization, migration path, lifecycle/retention, reset/deletion, concurrency, isolation, auditability, contribution/lineage/idempotency preservation — with unresolved securities kept `UNRESOLVED` and the certified reference portfolio provably untouched (`G-2` §6/§7). *(CV-20 → Q1 → WP-4b → WP-5.)*
6a. **Reports coherence is judged separately** (CV-21): a stated boundary, or its own `R1`/`R2`/`R3`/`F-7` evidence. Portfolio admission never satisfies it.
6b. **Coverage:** for whatever Q3 selects, the accepted universe is stated and enforced identically across surfaces, with the real-name ceiling (52 of 2,250 under option B) disclosed rather than discovered by the user. *(CV-22.)*
7. No repository front-matter contradicts verified state (XV-1), and no gate record leaves an orphan "pending" where a disposition exists (§16.4.3 rows).
8. Cross-repo posture unchanged where protected: IRR fences byte-identical — `git hash-object` on `frontend/server/reports-transport.ts` = `981470541812a64c733309f1debec75455d85bee`, `frontend/server/user-portfolio-transport.ts` = `51704c12a467cd2f718a030b5036bc55c9afa73f`, `frontend/server/owner-context-unavailable.ts` = `cb8db45d668d28cee7e0a52721001d579a872733` (all three re-verified at `CV-CORR-01` on `2026-10-14`), tested 2F blobs unchanged, IPD refs unmoved unless an act says otherwise.

## 16.9 Scope exclusions and boundaries that must stay protected

Out of scope and **not** a prerequisite for anything above (per `H-18` and `GC-P2-SCOPE-CORR-01`): global authentication, OIDC/Keycloak, RBAC, multi-user and tenant infrastructure, enterprise authentication architecture, production activation, provider entitlements/live feeds.

Protected by existing authority, unchanged by this section: no re-opening of `REG-01…REG-13` dispositions; `GC-D1` operational prohibitions, `GC-D2` (2F) and `GC-D3…GC-D6` records stand as written; `P2`/`P3` admission, `AD-20` residual items, `D115-A` ineffectiveness, "no Company Identity Authority"; `/api/user-portfolios*` and `/api/reports*` stay `503`; `/api/pit/company/:companyId` stays unadmitted (`P4`); `phase14.1-recovery-deposit@c115d1f4` and the historical `program-v1.1.0` LTS stay preserved; frozen engine/calibration/certification artifacts are untouched; the retired auth/admin stack on stale branches must not re-enter `main` (XV-10); IPD `main` receives no promotion, re-pin or lineage merge without its own act.

## 16.10 Verification limitations and unresolved contradictions

1. **Windows/PowerShell evidence domain not entered.** P-2 establishment, `F5.2` history, the browser acceptance records and every `operator-transferred` result remain as recorded; this session verified their **artifacts and reconciliation** (blob/SHA/ancestry per §§15.19–15.22) but did not re-execute Windows steps. No Windows claim is made here.
2. **The retained P-2 transcript is deliberately incomplete.** Its manifest-declared coverage omits host identity, Block A pin verification, ACLs, tooling hashes, D1/D1b and F2–F4, and it carries a permanent `git diff --check` validation exception. It is accepted as-is under `GC-P2-DEPOSIT-DISPOSITION-01`; nothing in this section upgrades it.
3. **Dangling objects and other archives not searched** — consistent with §12.1's stated boundary ("no claim of proof of non-existence beyond the search boundary"). Absence of an authorizing act for `a0ab5a34`/PR #42 is not re-litigated here.
4. **`G24 84/84`** and other IPD-side suite counts cited in earlier records were **not** re-run in this session (IPD was read-only at `main` only). V10's 542-pass figure is IPD **`main`**, not G24.
5. **Unresolved contradiction preserved, not resolved:** the Gate C packet (C) treats `companyId` canonicity as load-bearing and conflicting with `D115/D-2` findings, while the admitted PIT adapter maps `companyId`→`ISIN:<isin>:EQ` locally for reads only. Both statements stand as recorded; CV-20's decision must say which model governs *owner* data (reads remain inside `AD-02`).
6. **Install reproducibility caveat:** V1 succeeded only after exporting the sandbox CA bundle; treat that failure as environment-specific (XV-11). The durable fact is the GitHub-network and branch-ref dependency (XV-4).
7. **Not re-derived here:** any prior "verified" claim whose evidence remains applicable was reused, not repeated — `E2E-030`, NP-13, NP-08/D08, NP-12, `F-1/F-2/F-3` browser acceptance, `REG-07/08/09`; and, for `CV-CORR-01`, all of V1–V14 (no input to them changed).
8. **Read this session, not re-derived:** `G-2` was re-read in full at `main` for §16.13 and is `ACCEPTED — ARCHITECTURAL DIRECTION RECORDED` with a §11 sign-off (`Ramki`, 2026-09-30, recording baseline `IRR main@19b42e71`); that acceptance is the only basis on which CV-20 is written. Separately, `H-18-PRODUCT-SCOPE-SINGLE-USER-PERSONAL-USE-01` still carries its original `CANDIDATE — PENDING PROGRAM AUTHORITY APPROVAL AND MERGE AUTHORIZATION` banner although the record is on `main`, its own effectiveness condition (merged + independently verified) appears satisfied by that presence, and `GC-P2-SCOPE-CORR-01` operates on its content. **This plan does not adjudicate that banner**; it is flagged for the PA beside CV-33/CV-34 hygiene, and the exclusions it states are treated as operative because the PA restated them directly at §15.19.

## 16.11 What still stands between today and "coherent and usable"

Short answer: **three small independent fixes make the verified product operable and honest; two capability questions and one authority act decide what the product may become.** No single item blocks the application.

1. Anyone can install and run the *research* product today, but must reverse-engineer how (CV-30/CV-31), and two sidebar headings land on "not yet built" pages (CV-41).
2. **User Portfolios** are governed by an accepted `G-2` architecture whose non-production implementation **was authorized and completed** on 2026-10-07 but is **unadmitted and un-wired**: `G-2` §9’s withhold gave way only to that act (V16), IPD `main`’s store is in-memory (V15), and the only durable candidate sits on a branch `149` commits behind `main` (V17) whose one binding path is closed by scope (CV-50 — terminal record, not a prerequisite and not a gate). What is missing is the **next act in order** — authorize qualification, record its result, and only then decide admission as its own act — not a debate about ownership and not an admission shortcut.
3. **Reports** has a fence but no capability, and its deferral is its own (`REG-11` Option C, `G-2` §8 excludes it from the portfolio decision) — it should become a stated product boundary or its own scoped item (CV-21).
4. **Coverage** is the quietest and most user-visible gap: durable surfaces can only name 13 governed rows, so "add RELIANCE" fails (CV-22), and analytics are a frozen `SNAPSHOT` by certified design.
5. Two integrity traps and a stale-paper trail: an install that breaks if a branch moves (CV-25), a test command that can pass with zero tests (CV-35), stale front-matter (CV-33/XV-1) and a stale IPD program tracker (CV-36).

After 1 and 5 (WP-1, WP-2, WP-3) the application is **coherent**: what it shows is honest, what it claims is verified, and every open boundary is stated as a boundary. It becomes **complete for personal use** only when item 2's sequence (and, if wanted, items 3 and 4) is carried out under its own authorities.

## 16.15 Correction record `CV-CORR-03` (`2026-10-14`; consistency corrections only — no re-audit, no new evidence claims)

**Two defects corrected, both in text this PR itself introduced; nothing else was touched.**

1. **`CV-50` accounting.** §16.12 and §19 counted `CV-50` — which §16.4.4 and §18 describe as a terminal path closed by scope, with no dependants and nothing scheduled — as one of `3` blocked items inside `23` outstanding pending-work items, i.e. as actionable. Now: the register group keeps `3` blocked rows (`CV-50`, `CV-51`, `CV-52`) so no governed status is altered, while §16.12 splits it into **blocked-active `2`** and **terminal, scope-closed `1`**, and adds **active outstanding work `22`** (`6 + 12 + 2 + 2`) against **recorded outstanding `23`**; §19’s reconciliation, its User-Portfolio row heading and its Deferred/OOS ID list carry the same distinction. No status was changed to make the arithmetic work — `CV-50` stays `BLOCKED`, stays in §16.4.4 and §18 row 33, and stays visible in §19 as a record rather than as work.
2. **Portfolio admission sequence.** §16.7.1 Q1 offered **"B. Admit as-is with a dated acceptance note"**, and §20 Stage 4 offered "admit" as an authority choice, while §20 Stage 5 required implementation, qualification and admission as separate acts and `D-1` plus the implementation record §8.5 prohibit admission. That contradiction is removed: Q1 now offers **(A)** authorize qualification only, **(B)** qualification plus a bounded residual implementation act, **(C)** dated deferral — with the removed option recorded explicitly as *deliberately not offered*, citing `D-1`, record §8.5 and the `AD-20`/Gate A + `P2`/`P3` qualification bar — and Stage 4’s exit criteria now state that the act "grants no implementation authority by implication" and that "admission remains ungranted until its own act". Stage 5 carries separate **5a qualification** and **5b admission** exit criteria, with 5a-without-5b named as a legitimate end state, and CV-20, CV-50’s dependency wording, §16.5 (both cells and the graph), WP-4, WP-4b, WP-5, Q1a, §16.11 and §17 were aligned to the same ordering. The duplicate bullet that Q1 had acquired across `CV-CORR-01`/`CV-CORR-02` ("Not options" and "Not an available option") was merged into one.

**Unchanged by this correction:** the `51`-row matrix, the `13`-row summary structure, the `8`-stage progression, every register status, `G-2`/`D-1`/`D-2`/`H-18`/`P2`–`P8`/`AD-20`/`REG-*`/`GC-*` records, all fences and their hashes, the `20` executed V-rows and every prior claim of executed evidence. No product code, route, UI, pin, IPD ref or decision record was touched; no suite was re-run; nothing was merged.

## 17 Executive Convergence View (`2026-10-14`; grounds §§18–20; derived from §16.3 V1–V20 and §16.4)

*Read this first, then §18 (capability matrix — one row per capability, the exact 17 fixed columns), §19 (every outstanding register item in one place) and §20 (the ordered progression). These four sections add no decision, lift no fence, authorize no implementation and duplicate no register text: they re-express §16 in the requested structure, sharpened by `CV-CORR-02` (§16.14).*

**Working and accepted today (protected — do not redo).** The application runs end-to-end as a single-owner, credential-free workbench: executive dashboard with its certified DTO; the portfolio workspace over the certified reference portfolio; company, cross-sector, decision-matrix and engine-registry surfaces over the 13 certified sector engines; the governed screener (POST-only, 13 server-derived members, `422` against client-supplied membership); evidence landing, explorer and replay at `:id`; watchlists, collaboration and settings durable on the Class-C append-only journal; the owner-context carrier with its fail-closed `503`; the `G-2` §5 user-portfolio HTTP contract, port, `D-2` §8 translation boundary and the two read surfaces with their tests; the PIT read seam answering `/api/pit/market-data` against the pinned IPD package; the IPD data plane and offline shell; `REG-01`…`REG-13`; and the `program-v1.2.0` tag plus GitHub Release. Executed here: `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors, `64` files / `703` tests passing, `npm run build` `256.73 kB`, `13/13` live route probes `200`, journal append observed on disk, IPD `542/542` — §16.4.1 (CV-01…CV-11) on evidence V2–V8, V12–V14.

**Implemented but not integrated or wired.** (1) The `G-2` durable portfolio line: a complete, remotely verified non-production implementation exists — contract, port, translation boundary, G24 HTTP adapter, transport, `59` tests — but it is reachable **only** from `origin/arena/a2df3b85-iips-review-recovered`, `7` ahead and **`149` behind** `main`, and its own record §8.5 withholds main admission (V16/V17); `ipdUserPortfolioAdapter.ts` and `contractStub.ts` do not exist on `main`. (2) `frontend/server/d115-runtime.ts`: runtime `CompanyId` boundary with tests, referenced by nothing but its own test (V19, CV-43). (3) `iips-platform` snapshots and `WorkflowRuntime`: consumed by platform code and one regression test only, never by a product route (V19). (4) IPD `main` has **no HTTP server at all** and `0` files under `src/persistence` (V15/V18), so nothing on IPD `main` can serve a durable portfolio store. (5) Macro/D08 and the PIT seam are API-only: no UI feature directory exists for `macro`, `pit`, `reports` or a real `intelligence` surface (V19).

**Incomplete, data-limited or awaiting validation.** Six nav destinations land on "not yet built" placeholders (`/research`, `/research/sector/:id`, `/evidence/snapshots`, `/evidence/replay` landing, `/intelligence/*`, wildcard) while the sidebar promises capability — CV-41. Owner-scoped writes can only address the `13`-row governed universe (real ISINs answer `404 governed-row-not-found`) and reference analytics are a frozen `SNAPSHOT` — CV-22/XV-9. IPD’s broker-ingestion `PortfolioStore` is in-memory, so nothing survives a restart — CV-39/V15. The journal’s separate-process restart proof is off-`main` — CV-38/V17. IPD’s `npm test` can report `0` tests and exit `0` — CV-35. **Neither repository carries a CI workflow**, so every "tests pass" statement in this program is local-execution evidence — CV-42 (new). The runtime pin is reachable through exactly one branch ref — CV-25. Documentation still contradicts the shipped release state, and the IPD XLSX tracker reads `59/59 NOT STARTED` against `evidence/p13…p17` — CV-33/CV-36.

**Decisions and authorities still outstanding (PA-owned; nothing else can substitute).** (1) Whether to authorize the **qualification** act for the already-implemented, unadmitted durable line at current `main` — alone, or together with a bounded residual implementation act — or to defer it with a dated reason; **"admit as-is" is not offered**, because `D-1` and the implementation record §8.5 prohibit admission and it remains a separate later act resting on recorded qualification. (2) Designate (or decline) the **non-authentication ownership source** the `503` user-portfolio seam waits on — the transport states it does not authenticate, synthesize a principal, pass credentials upstream or infer a tenant. (3) Within IPD ownership, which durable storage technology satisfies `G-2` §3, and how §3’s owner-identity, isolation, auditability and durability requirements are read for a single-owner non-production application (XV-13) — the substance of §3 is not waivable. (4) Reports: `REG-11` Option C stands until an own disposition act names an ownership source, contract shape and qualification (`R1`/`R2`/`R3`/`F-7`). (5) Governed-universe scope for owner data (CV-22); `Gate A` necessity now that its only consumer was removed (CV-23); `F6_INVENTORY_AUTHORIZED` and `TD-7b` (CV-24); the pin-durability act (CV-25); the `D115` disposition (CV-43).

**Blockers specific to User Portfolio.** The absence of an authorized qualification act for the durable line (`G-2` §9’s withhold gave way only to the 2026-10-07 non-production implementation, V16), and — strictly after it — a separate admission act, since `D-1`’s standing prohibition on G24 main admission and the implementation record §8.5 are lifted only by that act and never by qualification, planning or a passing suite; the deferred live-IdP and per-user-credential acts that keep the seam at `503` (record §8.1–§8.2); no durable sink on IPD `main` (V15/V18); and the `149`-commit staleness of the only branch carrying the adapter (V17). One path inside this chain is **closed, not pending**: `P-4`→`P-6`→`P-8` exists solely to satisfy the G24 store’s OIDC-verified credential binding, which `H-18` and `GC-P2-SCOPE-CORR-01` exclude (CV-50) — nothing else waits on it.

**Blockers specific to Reports.** Its own `REG-11` Option C deferral; no ownership source (`R1`) and no contract shape (`R2`); consumer admission (`R3`) and qualification (`F-7`, including restart durability) unmet; and the unresolved `H-4`/`H-16` `D-1`↔PR #42 conflict, which only PA errata can settle. `G-2` §8 excludes Reports from the portfolio decision, so no portfolio outcome unblocks it — and no Reports outcome blocks the portfolio (CV-21/CV-51).

**Issues that independently affect other capabilities** (each actionable without any portfolio or Reports decision): pin durability CV-25; IPD false-green runner CV-35 and the absent CI in both repositories CV-42; governed-universe limits CV-22; documentation and tracker contradictions CV-33/CV-34/CV-36/CV-37; nav honesty CV-41; runbook and start command CV-30/CV-31; off-`main` restart proof CV-38; `D115` disposition CV-43.

**Next authorized action, and what proceeds in parallel.** Immediately authorized, no decision needed: **§20 Stage 1 (runbook + start command), in parallel with Stage 2 (pin-durability protection) and Stage 3 (validation-integrity and documentation closure)** — that is the whole distance between "verified repository" and "usable application" for everything already working. The portfolio chain’s next act is a **decision, not work**: §20 Stage 4. Reports (§20 Stage 6), universe coverage (Stage 7) and the hygiene stages run alongside it and are not queued behind it. In §18 the only rows whose release-readiness depends on the portfolio decision are 30–33, on Reports 35–37, and on the universe decision 41; every other row is independent — no workstream is, or may be described as, the sole blocker to convergence.


## 18 Capability Convergence Matrix (exact 17-column structure; one row per capability or cross-repository workstream)

**How to read.** Every cell judges **one dimension** independently and is never carried over from a neighbouring cell: implementation is not proof of integration, wiring, validation, acceptance, qualification or release readiness, and a passing automated suite is not product E2E or formal acceptance. Work-item detail stays in the register (§16.4); rows reference `CV-*`/`XV-*` IDs instead of restating it. `V1`…`V20` are the executed checks in §16.3 and §16.14 and are the primary anchors; `NA` is used only where the dimension genuinely cannot apply.

**Status legend.** Workbook vocabulary: `V` VERIFIED (executed or directly inspected here) · `I` IMPLEMENTED · `P` PARTIAL · `NI` NOT INTEGRATED (exists, no consumer on the authoritative ref) · `NW` NOT WIRED (no route or UI) · `NP` NOT PERSISTED · `VP` VALIDATION PENDING · `EP` E2E PENDING · `EM` EVIDENCE MISSING · `AP` AUTHORITY PENDING · `BL` BLOCKED · `UR` UNRESOLVED · `NA` NOT APPLICABLE · `OOS` OUT OF SCOPE. Extra values kept distinct because the vocabulary alone would merge materially different states: `DEFERRED` (a dated decision says so) · `NOT SATISFIED` (a named bar exists and is not met) · `NOT AUTHORIZED` / `AUTHORITY WITHHELD` (an express prohibition in a governed record) · `RECOVERY UNPROVEN`, written `VP — … off-main` where a proof exists but not on `main` · `SOURCE-REPORTED` (asserted by a record this session did not re-execute — e.g. the Windows-only shell acceptance, the `G24` `84/84` live suite, the v1.1 certificates). A `SOURCE-REPORTED` cell must not be re-labelled `V` without executing it.

## 20 Ordered Convergence Progression (dependency-ordered; no stage blocks an unrelated capability)

**Rule.** Stages 1–3 and 7 are **authorized now**; Stages 4–6 are **decision gates** whose planning is authorized while their implementation is not — planning a stage never authorizes building it, and the only authority source is the record named in "Authority Required". The one ordering constraint the evidence supports is `Stage 4 → Stage 5`, and inside Stage 5 the order is fixed too: implementation (only if authorized) → qualification → admission by its own act; a qualified-but-unadmitted line is a legitimate end state for this cycle. Nothing makes Reports, universe coverage, pin durability, CI, the runbook or documentation wait behind the portfolio decision, so they run in parallel; §16.5’s four chains, not a single critical path, are the real shape.

**Stage-to-package cross-check.** `8` stages map onto the register’s `WP-1`, `WP-2`, `WP-3`, `WP-4` (Q1/Q2/Q3 plus the ownership-source designation), `WP-4b`, `WP-5`, `WP-6`: `WP-4b` is absorbed into Stage 4 because V16 showed its intended output already exists, and Stage 5/6 split `WP-5` by capability — which is why §16.7 lists `7` packages while §20 lists `8` stages. No stage re-runs completed evidence; each names the record that would have to change before it could move.
